Parametric sensitivity analysis demonstrates that distributed environmental factors, such as soil moisture and line sag, act as uniform magnitude offsets. Conversely, the conductor's magnetic permeability and local injection-transformer impedances dictate the channel's resonant spectral shape. Furthermore, cross-brand analysis proves that utilizing generic transformer models introduces significant prediction errors, confirming that accurate simulation requires manufacturer- and tap-specific data. Validated against in-situ field measurements from three transmitters, this digital twin replicates the path loss and dominant frequency-selective fading of the physical grid. Yielding a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) between 4.65 dB and 9.73 dB across the three transmit paths, the model provides a practically reliable framework for deploying NB-PLC across rural SWER infrastructure.

</details>


### [243] [Dispersion-aware Localization Network for Wideband OFDM Pinching-Antenna Integrated Sensing and Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20731)
*Hyeonho Noh,Hyun Jong Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段ISAC框架，通过通信感知协同的波束成形与天线布局优化，结合物理模型驱动的色散感知定位网络（DiPL-Net），有效抑制波导管色散导致的距离估计退化问题，在保持通信速率的同时显著提升目标定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINCHING天线系统在宽带OFDM场景下因波导管非线性群延迟效应引发色散，严重劣化距离估计性能，亟需突破通信与感知联合优化的技术瓶颈。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过分数规划波束成形器和坐标下降法天线布局，在感知波束模式增益约束下最大化下行链路总速率；第二阶段构建基于物理距离字典的色散感知得分图，利用双核残差卷积块（短核匹配OFDM主瓣、长核匹配色散尾迹）实现色散去卷积的目标定位。

Result: 仿真表明，该方案相较传统感知基线方法取得显著定位增益，同时维持可达通信速率不变。

Conclusion: 验证了所提框架通过物理模型嵌入深度学习网络，在解决波导管色散难题的同时实现了通感一体化系统的高效协同。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASS) provide a large effective aperture and substantial path-loss reduction at low hardware cost, making them attractive for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). Under wideband OFDM operation, however, the antennas on each waveguide impose nonlinear, position-dependent group delays on the same baseband signal, giving rise to waveguide dispersion that severely degrades range estimation. To this end, this paper proposes a two-stage ISAC framework comprising communication-aware beamforming and antenna placement followed by a dispersion-aware target localization stage. A fractional-programming beamformer and an element-wise coordinate-descent placement jointly maximize the downlink sum rate under a sensing beampattern-gain constraint, and the resulting optimized beamformer and placement determine the effective sensing channel used by the localization stage. The proposed DisPersion-aware Localization Network (DiPL-Net) detects targets from the received signal via a dispersion-aware score map built on a physics-derived range dictionary. Its convolutional backbone employs dual-kernel residual blocks, each pairing a short kernel matched to the OFDM main lobe with a long kernel matched to the dispersion tail, so as to deconvolve the dispersion and restore a sharp target peak at each true target. Simulation results show substantial localization gains over various sensing baselines while preserving the achievable communication rate.

</details>


### [244] [Electromagnetic Twin: Completing the Wireless World from Sparse Channel Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20813)
*Tuo Wu,Jie Tang,Kangda Zhi,Junteng Yao,Maged Elkashlan,Kin-Fai Tong,George K. Karagiannidis,Jinhong Yuan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出“电磁孪生”系统，利用稀疏信道数据和场景信息更新信道知识图以重构无线状态，显著优于传统插值方法，但视觉语义特征对性能提升的贡献未获证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集信道信息获取成本高，现有地图需随环境变化刷新，亟需一种能基于稀疏证据高效重构无线状态的可更新数字表示。

Method: 构建“电磁孪生”，以CKM为内存结合场景信息，在证据更新后再生成输出。采用学习到的RF补全骨干恢复传播结构，并引入轻量级残差适配器验证冻结CLIP特征的有效性。

Result: 在4%测量点位及55%语义缺失下，RF骨干RMSE达4.44 dB，优于CKM插值（8.29 dB）和物理先验（8.38 dB）。残差适配降低RMSE 0.135 dB，但与随机特征控制在统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 验证了测量-更新-查询循环及轻量级残差校正的有效性，但鉴于随机特征表现相当，不支持将性能提升归因于视觉语义特征。

Abstract: Acquiring dense channel information over many locations and beams incurs considerable pilot and processing overhead. Radio maps and channel knowledge maps (CKMs) reduce this overhead by reusing site-specific channel information, but their contents must be refreshed when new measurements or environmental observations become available. This paper introduces an \emph{electromagnetic twin} as an updatable digital representation that uses sparse channel evidence to reconstruct the wireless state requested by communication queries. Rather than replacing radio maps or CKMs, the twin uses a CKM as channel memory, combines it with registered scene information, and regenerates its outputs after each evidence update. We instantiate this idea by completing a two-dimensional channel-gain field from sparse samples and an incomplete floor plan. A learned RF completion backbone recovers the main propagation structure, and a lightweight residual adapter tests whether frozen CLIP features provide useful side information. With $4\%$ measured locations and $55\%$ missing semantic objects, the RF backbone attains $4.44$ dB RMSE, compared with $8.29$ dB for CKM interpolation and $8.38$ dB for an incomplete physics prior. Residual adaptation reduces RMSE by a paired mean of $0.135$ dB (95\% confidence interval: $0.100$--$0.169$ dB), but a same-capacity random-feature control is statistically indistinguishable from the CLIP-conditioned adapter. The results therefore support the measurement--update--query loop and lightweight residual correction, while avoiding an unsupported attribution of the correction to visual semantics.

</details>


### [245] [From Sparse Probes to Sum-Rate Maximization: Electromagnetic Twin Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20846)
*Tuo Wu,Kangda Zhi,Jie Tang,Jianchao Zheng,Naofal Al-Dhahir,Fumiyuki Adachi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种电磁孪生（ET）波束成形方法，通过稀疏空间探测实现高效波束优化，仅需1%随机探测即可达到2.69 bit/s/Hz，7%感知探测可达到3.03 bit/s/Hz，接近完美协方差波束成形的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每次无线地图更新时探测每个候选位置成本过高，需要一种能够将稀疏空间探测转换为有效波束决策的低成本方法。

Method: ET波束成形存储位置相关的角功率谱，通过图正则化估计更新创新，查询用户协方差进行投影波束优化，并利用速率敏感性边界通过查询加权的后验方差减小来选择后续探测位置。

Result: 使用1%随机探测，ET波束成形达到2.69 bit/s/Hz，而静态信道知识地图仅为1.12 bit/s/Hz；使用7%感知探测，达到3.03 bit/s/Hz，仅比完美协方差波束成形低3.4%。

Conclusion: ET波束成形方法能够在极稀疏探测条件下实现接近最优的波束成形性能，为无线通信系统的低成本高效波束优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Probing every candidate location at each wireless-map update is costly. This letter develops electromagnetic-twin (ET) beamforming that converts sparse spatial probes into a sum-rate decision. The ET stores a location-dependent angular power spectrum, updates its innovation through graph-regularized estimation, and queries user covariances for projected beam optimization. A rate-sensitivity bound selects subsequent probes by query-weighted posterior-variance reduction. With 1\% random probes, ET beamforming achieves $2.69$ bit/s/Hz versus $1.12$ for a static channel knowledge map; with 7\% query-aware probes, it reaches $3.03$ bit/s/Hz, within 3.4\% of perfect-covariance beamforming.

</details>


### [246] [SR-TL1: A Square-Root TL1-Norm Framework for Robust SMV DoA Estimation under Highly-Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20943)
*Youval Klioui*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出SR-TL1稀疏恢复框架，结合平方根LASSO与变换L1范数，用于高相干过完备字典下存在角度相关阵列缺陷的单测量向量DoA估计，实现了抗噪声方差鲁棒性、强恢复性能与计算效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对高相干过完备字典与角度相关阵列缺陷下的DoA估计挑战，需要一种既能抵抗噪声方差影响又能在相干条件下实现强恢复性能的稀疏估计方法。传统L1范数方法在高相干场景下恢复性能受限，且对噪声方差敏感。

Method: 提出SR-TL1框架：1) 结合抗噪声方差的平方根LASSO与强恢复性能的非凸变换L1范数(TL1-norm)；2) 采用凸差算法(DCA)与交替方向乘子法(ADMM)获得闭式更新规则；3) 利用字典Gram矩阵的低秩特性实现高效计算，每轮复杂度O(MN)。

Result: 实验验证显示：1) 正则化超参数对噪声方差的敏感性降低；2) 相比最先进基线方法具有竞争性恢复性能；3) 提供DCA迭代的收敛性保证；4) 计算复杂度每轮迭代最多O(MN)，其中M为阵列规模，N为字典长度。

Conclusion: SR-TL1框架通过创新性地结合两种优势特性，在高相干过完备字典与阵列缺陷条件下实现了鲁棒、高效且性能优越的DoA估计，具有理论收敛保证与实用计算效率。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Square-Root Transformed $L_1$-norm ($SR\text{-}TL_{1}$) sparse recovery framework for single-measurement-vector (SMV) direction of arrival (DoA) estimation under highly-coherent overcomplete dictionaries with angular-dependent array imperfections. The proposed framework combines the square-root Least Absolute Shrinkage and Selection Operator (square-root LASSO) framework which is known to be robust against noise variance with the Transformed $L_1$-norm ($TL_1$-norm), a non-convex penalty that shows a stronger recovery performance than the classical convex $L_1$-norm under highly-coherent dictionaries. We use the Difference of Convex Algorithm (DCA) along with the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) algorithm to obtain simple, closed-form update rules and provide an efficient implementation that leverages the low-rank nature of the Gram matrix of the dictionary so as to obtain a computational complexity of at most $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ per iteration where $M$ is the array size and $N$ is the length of the dictionary. We additionally provide a convergence guarantee for the DCA iterates of $SR\text{-}TL_{1}$. Experimental verification of the proposed framework shows a lower sensitivity of the regularization hyperparameter to the noise variance level and a competitive recovery performance compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [247] [Obstacle-Aware Online Receiver Planning in Multistatic Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21013)
*Jingwei Hu,Dave Zachariah,Petre Stoica,Torbjörn Wigren*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非短视的滚动时域框架用于多基站追踪，通过凸碰撞避免约束和保持视距信号条件的控制目标，有效解决障碍物环境下的移动接收机轨迹规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多基站测距配合移动接收机因自适应传感器几何结构可提供良好追踪性能，但含信号遮挡障碍物的环境中，需要接收机轨迹规划以维持与发射机及目标的视距条件。

Method: 开发非短视滚动时域框架，采用凸碰撞避免约束，控制目标侧重于维持良好视距信号条件，并考虑未来障碍物影响。

Result: 通过数值实验验证了方法的计算效率和追踪准确性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效在复杂障碍物环境中实现高效准确的追踪，同时保障良好的视距通信条件。

Abstract: Multistatic ranging with mobile receivers enables good tracking performance due to the combined adaptive sensor geometry. However, environments that contain signal obstructing obstacles require receiver trajectory planning to maintain line-of-sight (LOS) conditions with transmitters and the target of interest. In this letter, we develop a non-myopic receding-horizon framework for multistatic tracking. It uses convex collision-avoidance constraints and a control objective that focuses on maintaining good LOS signal conditions, taking into account future obstructions. We demonstrate the efficiency and tracking accuracy of the method via a numerical experiment.

</details>


### [248] [Frequency-Selective Pinching-Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21020)
*Chongjun Ouyang,Hao Jiang,Zhiguo Ding,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究采用分段介质波导的上行夹紧天线系统（PASS），分析由于大孔径波导引起的差分传播时延导致的频率选择性信道，推导Toeplitz块模型，刻画无限长块和有限长块传输下的可达速率，提出感知频率选择性的PA放置优化算法，结果表明该方法显著优于频率平坦近似下的放置方案，尤其在大带宽和大孔径场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模波导部署中，差分传播时延会引入显著的频率选择性，若忽略该效应，频率平坦（FF）模型会高估可达速率，导致性能损失。因此需要准确建模频率选择性信道，并设计对应的PA放置优化方法以提升系统性能。

Method: 1. 推导表征频率选择性信道的Toeplitz块模型；2. 分析无限长块传输下联合与并行解码的单用户及多用户可达速率；3. 研究FF速率高估与时延扩展及波导孔径的关系；4. 在循环前缀单载波频域均衡框架下推导有限长块可达速率；5. 设计基于逐元素算法的FS感知PA放置优化方法。

Result: 1. 频率平坦模型会高估精确的FS速率；高估量在小时延扩展下近似与时延扩展的平方成正比，在大孔径 regime 下至少以双重对数速率增长。2. 有限长块可达速率得到推导。3. 提出的FS感知PA放置算法在两种传输体制下均能显著提升可达速率，尤其在大带宽和大孔径条件下，相对于基于FF近似的放置方案优势明显。

Conclusion: 大规模波导部署中的差分传播时延会引起严重的频率选择性，不能简单使用频率平坦模型；通过感知频率选择性的PA放置优化，可以更准确地预测速率并显著提升系统性能，该结论对宽带、大孔径通信系统的部署与设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: An uplink pinching-antenna system (PASS) is considered, where a segmented dielectric waveguide is employed to serve multiple users and one pinching antenna (PA) is activated on each segment. Extensive waveguide deployment reduces user-to-PA path loss but also creates differential propagation delays among the PA-assisted paths spanning the large waveguide aperture. These delays are retained, and a Toeplitz block model is derived to characterize the resulting frequency-selective (FS) channel. For infinite-blocklength transmission, the achievable single-user rate and the multiuser sum-rates under joint and parallel decoding are characterized. For carrier-phase-aligned single-user PA placement, the frequency-flat (FF) model is shown to overestimate the exact FS rate. The difference between the rates computed under the FF channel model and the exact FS channel model is referred to as the FF rate overestimation, and its dependence on the waveguide-aperture-induced delay spread is characterized. For small delay spreads, the FF rate overestimation is shown to increase approximately quadratically with the delay spread, whereas a derived lower bound reveals that the overestimation grows at least double logarithmically with the waveguide aperture, or equivalently with the delay spread, in the large-aperture regime. For finite-blocklength transmission, achievable rates are derived under a practical cyclic-prefix single-carrier frequency-domain equalization framework. An element-wise algorithm is developed to optimize FS-aware PA placement in both transmission regimes. The analytical findings demonstrate that differential delays across the extensively deployed waveguide can cause significant frequency selectivity and that the proposed FS-aware PA placement method substantially improves the achievable rate over placement based on the FF approximation, especially for large bandwidths and apertures.

</details>


### [249] [HAP-Centric Flying Ad-Hoc Networks With Cell-Free Non-Terrestrial Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21038)
*Muhammet Kırık,Liza Afeef,Halim Yanikomeroglu,Hüseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以高空平台(HAP)为中心的多层飞自组网(FANET)架构，将HAP与无人机(UAV)集成形成独立无蜂窝非地面系统，克服现有NTN卫星中心设计的局限性，为6G NTN部署提供灵活、可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前非地面网络(NTN)设计过于依赖卫星中心和陆地蜂窝网络假设，限制了网络灵活性和可扩展性。随着6G发展，需要更灵活的空天网络架构来实现广泛覆盖和快速部署，同时保持系统自主运行能力。

Method: 提出三层HAP-centric FANET架构：1) HAP间自组网层；2) HAP-UAV协作层；3) UAV-地面接入层。分析各层挑战并引入关键技术策略，包括快速波束对准、非协调FANET/陆地网络共存、用户定位和环境分类。通过三个案例研究验证系统性能。

Result: 研究结果表明HAP-centric FANET架构能够实现空中连接、自适应覆盖和干扰感知用户接入。三个案例研究验证了系统的可行性，并提出了标准化路径。结果显示该系统具备高数据速率连接、异质环境下用户接入等关键能力。

Conclusion: HAP-centric FANET为非地面网络提供了一种新的范式，通过HAP与UAV的协同工作实现独立运行的无蜂窝系统。该架构为6G NTN部署奠定了基础，展现出在弹性、可扩展性和应用导向方面的优势，有望推动下一代空天网络技术的发展。

Abstract: High-altitude platforms (HAPs) are key enablers of next-generation non-terrestrial networks (NTNs), offering wide coverage, long endurance, and rapid deployment. Despite these advantages, current NTN designs remain satellite-centric and rely on terrestrial cellular assumptions, limiting flexibility and scalability. To overcome these limitations, this article proposes a multi-layer HAP-centric flying ad-hoc network (FANET). In this framework, HAPs are integrated with distributed uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) to form a standalone, cell-free (CF) non-terrestrial system capable of autonomous operation. The layered architecture consists of an inter-HAP ad-hoc layer, a HAP-to-UAV cooperative layer, and a UAV-to-ground access layer, collectively enabling aerial connectivity, adaptive coverage, and interference-aware user access. Unique challenges for each layer are analyzed, including inter-HAP connectivity, FANET co-existence with terrestrial networks (TNs), and user access under heterogeneous conditions. Moreover, the article introduces enabling strategies such as fast beam alignment for high data rate connectivity, uncoordinated FANET/TN co-existence, and user localization and environment classification. Validated by three case studies, the discussion also outlines standardization pathways. The results highlight HAP-centric FANETs as a foundation for resilient, scalable, and application-oriented 6G NTN deployments.

</details>


### [250] [UW-OCDM for Low-Altitude UAV Communication and Cooperative Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21050)
*Yi Tao,Zhen Gao,Ziwei Wan,Yuezu Lv,Hua Wang,Kaibin Huang,Sheng Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成感知与通信(UW-OCDM)波形，用于高空无人机(UAV)场景，在保持低复杂度频域均衡的同时，实现了可靠的通信和高动态场景下的鲁棒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成感知与通信(ISAC)是低空经济中无人机的关键使能技术，需要解决高动态移动场景下的同步、信道估计和多目标检测跟踪等挑战。

Method: 采用将唯一字(UW)嵌入正交啁啾分频多址(OCDM)的UW-OCDM波形，通信方面利用UW进行时间同步和多普勒估计补偿，结合稀疏时空信道估计方法；感知方面利用确定性UW作为共享先验，构建分层多目标检测跟踪算法，包含直射路径干扰抑制、运动学预测、多候选筛选、离网格细化、残差验证和顺序干扰消除等步骤。

Result: 仿真结果表明在高动态UAV场景下实现了可靠通信和定位，同时保持了低复杂度频域均衡，降低了发射参考共享开销和多静态定位复杂度。

Conclusion: 所提出的UW-OCDM框架为高动态UAV场景提供了鲁棒的ISAC能力，有效平衡了性能与复杂度，为低空经济应用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) is a key enabler for uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) in the low-altitude economy. This paper proposes an ISAC waveform that embeds a unique word (UW) into orthogonal chirp division multiplexing (OCDM), termed UW-OCDM, together with corresponding communication reception and cooperative sensing schemes for high-mobility UAV scenarios. For communication, the embedded UW enables timing synchronization and Doppler estimation and compensation without requiring a separate synchronization sequence. A sparse spatio-temporal channel estimation method exploits the common channel support across multiple receive antennas and consecutive UW observations to support reliable data demodulation. For sensing, the deterministic UW serves as a shared prior that allows distributed base stations to construct sensing dictionaries locally without exchanging random payload symbols in real time. A hierarchical multi-target detection and tracking algorithm integrates direct-path interference suppression, kinematic prediction, multi-candidate screening, off-grid refinement, residual verification, and successive interference cancellation for robust localization with reduced search complexity. Simulation results demonstrate reliable communication and localization in highly dynamic UAV scenarios, while the proposed framework retains low-complexity frequency-domain equalization and reduces transmit-reference sharing overhead and multi-static localization complexity.

</details>


### [251] [Privacy-Preserving Localization via Transmit Antenna Selection and Permutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21064)
*Yiyang Zhang,Yanmo Hu,Junyuan Gao,Shuowen Zhang,Jiannong Cao,Liang Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于天线选择与排列的ISAC感知隐私保护方案，通过构建保密的导频-天线映射关系使合法接收方能唯一估计角度，而窃听者无法准确定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对6G网络中ISAC场景下感知隐私保护的空白问题。现有密码学主要保障信息隐私，但感知数据（如目标位置）的隐私保护尚缺乏有效方案，尤其在多天线系统中如何防止非法用户获取精确角度信息是关键挑战。

Method: 设计新型天线选择与排列传输策略：1) 选择部分天线子集进行激活；2) 对激活天线进行索引排列；3) 建立特定的导频-天线映射作为密钥。在无噪声理想条件下，系统能唯一确定满足Bob可解而Eve多义解的映射方案；在含噪声实际场景中，通过数值验证该方案可使Eve产生显著角度估计误差。

Result: 理论证明在无噪声时存在使Bob唯一估计AOD而Eve只能得到多个可能解的映射方案；仿真表明在加性高斯白噪声环境下，该方案能有效干扰Eve的角度估计精度，实现感知隐私保护。

Conclusion: 首次提出将天线映射关系作为物理层密钥的感知隐私保护机制，为6G ISAC系统中的安全感知提供了新范式。研究证实通过天线选择与排列可在保障合法用户定位精度的同时，有效防御窃听者的角度估计，填补了感知隐私保护的研究空白。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) has been identified as one primary usage scenario in the sixth-generation (6G) network. While techniques to preserve information privacy, such as cryptography, have been widely investigated, how to preserve sensing privacy is still an open problem in the literature. This paper makes an early attempt to tackle the above issue. Specifically, we consider a localization system consisting of a multi-antenna transmitter, termed Alice, a single-antenna legitimate receiver, termed Bob, and a single-antenna illegitimate receiver, termed Eve. To allow Bob to estimate Alice's angle-of-departure (AOD) but prevent Eve from performing this task based on Alice's signals, this paper proposes a novel antenna selection and permutation based transmission strategy for Alice. Under this scheme, Alice carefully selects a subset of antennas and permutes their indices to establish a specific pilot-antenna mapping for transmission. Similar to cryptography for information privacy, such a mapping will serve as the secret key to preserve localization privacy. In the special case without noise at Bob and Eve, we manage to find out all the antenna selection and permutation solutions such that with this key (knowledge about the exact pilot-antenna mapping), Bob can uniquely estimate Alice's AOD, while without this key, Eve can estimate multiple AODs of Alice that can lead to its received signals. In the noisy case, numerical results are provided to show that our scheme can confuse Eve to make inaccurate AOD estimation as well.

</details>


### [252] [Transmit Beamformer Design for Beampattern Synthesis in Phased-Array Radar Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21197)
*Berkan Kilic,Kenan Turbic,Slawomir Stanczak*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于波束图案匹配和干扰抑制的相控阵发射波束成形器设计方法，通过交替最小化算法和投影梯度下降法实现，并提供了理论收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相控阵波束成形器设计中需要同时实现波束图案匹配和干扰抑制两个目标，这对无线通信和雷达系统性能至关重要。传统方法难以有效联合优化这两个目标。

Method: 1. 提出联合优化框架同时处理波束匹配和干扰抑制两个目标；2. 使用交替最小化算法求解非凸问题，每个子问题具有闭式解；3. 将波束成形器设计重构为非凸集可行性问题；4. 采用具有理论收敛保证的投影梯度下降法求解，并结合加速技术提高收敛速度。

Result: 1. 其中一个子问题恢复了经典的增益最大化和零指向波束成形器；2. 提出的方法具有理论收敛保证；3. 大量仿真验证了方法的优异性能和计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在理论上保证了收敛性，在实践中具有计算效率，能够有效实现相控阵波束成形器的波束图案匹配和干扰抑制双重目标。

Abstract: This paper presents a phased-array transmit beamformer design for beampattern matching and interference suppression. We propose an optimization framework jointly addressing these two objectives. The resulting non-convex problem is solved via alternating minimization, where each subproblem admits a closed-form solution, one of which recovers the classical maximum-gain and null-steering phased-array beamformers. To design an algorithm ensuring theoretical convergence guarantees, the beamformer design is then reformulated as a non-convex set feasibility problem, closely related to the original formulation. This problem is addressed using a provably convergent projected gradient descent method and further enhanced with acceleration techniques that substantially improve empirical convergence. Extensive simulations validate the excellent performance and computational efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [253] [Pattern Multiplication Underestimates the Sidelobe Level of a Planar Slotted-Waveguide Array by 1.1 to 2.8 dB Across a 2% Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21231)
*William Khalili*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 验证槽波导阵列设计标准快捷法（单辐射单元解×阵列因子）与全波解差异：16×16槽阵列（WR-90，-30 dB泰勒分布）显示快捷法低估旁瓣电平，设计频率误差1.06 dB，2%频段边缘误差达2.22-2.84 dB（展宽1.78 dB），误差主要源于边缘单元；手册谐振长度公式偏差达37倍，Stevenson电导高估5.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估槽波导阵列设计惯用简化流程（单单元模式乘阵列因子）的精度，因该快捷法避免全波仿真但可能引入系统误差，尤其在频率敏感场景。

Method: 对WR-90波导中16×16槽平面阵列（-30 dB泰勒照明）进行全波仿真对比；通过13点单槽全波校准建立分析模型；结合网格收敛研究量化数值误差（±0.11 dB），并测试阵列尺寸缩放（8→16单元）对误差的影响。

Result: 快捷法在设计频率低估旁瓣1.06 dB，频段边缘低估2.22-2.84 dB（展宽>中频误差）；误差具符号性且边缘最显著；阵列加倍仅使误差从1.58 dB降至1.06 dB，证实边缘单元为主因；校准显示手册谐振长度公式变化3.9 μm vs 实测143.9 μm，Stevenson电导恒定高估5.16%。

Conclusion: 标准快捷法在频段边缘存在显著系统性误差（源于边缘单元互耦），不适用于高精度设计；传统手册公式（谐振长度、Stevenson电导）与实际偏差极大，需全波校准修正。

Abstract: The standard shortcut in slotted-waveguide array design -- solve one radiating stick, multiply its embedded pattern by the array factor of the intended aperture distribution, and accept the product as the array pattern -- is tested against a full-wave solution of the same metal. For a 16x16-slot planar array in WR-90 synthesised to a -30 dB Taylor illumination, the shortcut underestimates the transverse-plane sidelobe level by 1.06 dB at the design frequency and by 2.22 and 2.84 dB at the edges of a 2% band: a spread of 1.78 dB, larger than the mid-band error itself. Every sidelobe figure carries +/-0.11 dB of mesh spread from a convergence study on the production geometry. The error is signed, is worst at the band edges where the design has least margin, and falls only from 1.58 dB to 1.06 dB when the aperture is doubled from eight sticks to sixteen, which identifies the edge elements as its source. The array was made analysable by a thirteen-point single-slot full-wave calibration: over the design's offset range the handbook closed form for resonant length moves 3.9 micrometres where the measurement moves 143.9 micrometres, and Stevenson's conductance is shown to overestimate by a nearly constant 5.16%.

</details>
